- **line.ar**: كيف يعمل أمر ⁨LINE⁩ في أوتوكاد، مطالبةً بعد مطالبة: نقاط تُحدَّد أو تُكتب، و⁨Undo⁩ و⁨Close⁩، و⁨Enter⁩ للمتابعة من آخر خط. جرّبه مجانًا. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.ar.md))
- **line.cs**: Jak nakreslit úsečku v AutoCADu příkazem ÚSEČKA (LINE, zkratka U), výzva po výzvě: body určené nebo zadané, Zpět, Uzavři a navázání. Vyzkoušejte online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.cs.md))
- **line.de**: So arbeitet AutoCADs LINIE (LINE, Alias L): Punkte wählen oder tippen, Zurück und Schließen, und die Eingabetaste, um von der letzten Linie weiterzuzeichnen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.de.md))
- **line.es**: Cómo funciona LINEA (LINE, alias L) de AutoCAD en español, solicitud a solicitud: puntos señalados o escritos, desHacer y Cerrar. Pruébalo gratis online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.es.md))
- **line.fr**: Comment fonctionne LIGNE (LINE, alias L) d'AutoCAD en français, invite par invite : points cliqués ou tapés, annuler, clore, Entrée pour continuer. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.fr.md))
- **line.hu**: Az AutoCAD VONAL (LINE, VO) parancsa promptról promptra: kattintott vagy beírt pontok, Vissza és Bezárás, Enter az utolsó vonal folytatásához. Próbálja ki. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.hu.md))
- **line.it**: Come funziona LINEA (LINE, alias L) di AutoCAD in italiano, richiesta per richiesta: punti selezionati o digitati, Annulla e Chiudi, Invio per proseguire. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.it.md))
- **line.ja**: AutoCAD の LINE[線分](L)の使い方をプロンプトごとに解説します。点の指定と座標入力、元に戻す、閉じる、[Enter]で最後の線分から続けて作図。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.ja.md))
- **line.ko**: AutoCAD 선(LINE, L)을 프롬프트 순서대로 설명합니다: 클릭하거나 입력한 점, 명령 취소와 닫기, Enter 키로 마지막 선에서 이어 그리기. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.ko.md))
- **line.pl**: Jak narysować linię w AutoCADzie poleceniem LINIA (LINE, skrót L), monit po monicie: punkty wskazane lub wpisane, Cofaj, Zamknij i kontynuacja. Wypróbuj online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.pl.md))
- **line.pt-BR**: Como funciona o LINE do AutoCAD em português, LINHA (alias L), prompt a prompt: pontos indicados ou digitados, Desfazer e Fechar. Experimente grátis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.pt-BR.md))
- **line.ru**: Как начертить линию в AutoCAD командой ОТРЕЗОК (LINE), запрос за запросом: точки указанные или введенные, отмена, замыкание и продолжение от последней линии. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.ru.md))
- **line.zh-CN**: CAD 直线怎么画：AutoCAD LINE（直线，快捷键 L）按提示顺序讲解，拾取或输入点、放弃和闭合，以及按 Enter 键从上一条直线继续。在浏览器中免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.zh-CN.md))

- **lisa-project-ideation**: Generate persona-grounded, verifiable product ideas for the host project, then create PRDs for the selected build-ready ideas via lisa:research. First derives the personas the project actually serves (from its docs, code, data model, and releases — never invented), ideates per persona, and gates each idea on an obtainable data/source path and an empirical verification plan. Creates one PRD by default (top-ranked idea); max_prds widens the batch. prd_ready=true creates them prd-ready for auto-pickup by lisa:intake; default is draft for human review. ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-project-ideation.md))
- **lisa-pull-request-review**: Address and resolve PR review comments (human + bot): implement valid feedback, reply to invalid, resolve every thread. ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-pull-request-review.md))
- **lisa-queue-status**: Inspect the current project's PRD and build queues, summarize lifecycle counts, and surface actionable queue-health signals without mutating work. ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-queue-status.md))
- **lisa-repair-intake**: Repair counterpart to /lisa:intake. Vendor-agnostic batch scanner that finds stuck or half-closed work — items left in `blocked`, stalled in an in-progress role (build `claimed`, PRD `in_review`), terminal-labeled items still natively open, rollups whose children are all terminal, GitHub PRDs missing native child links, and GitHub issues missing official Lisa lifecycle labels — across the same queues /lisa:intake serves (Notion / Confluence / Linear / GitHub PRDs; JIRA / GitHub / Linear build issues). Repairs every materially actionable candidate inside the `max_candidates` cap: resumes stall… ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-repair-intake.md))
- **lisa-research**: Research a problem space and create a PRD in the configured PRD source. Investigates the codebase, defines user flows, assesses technical feasibility, synthesizes the spec, then creates it in the source (Notion / Confluence / GitHub / Linear) via lisa:prd-source-write — no loose document. prd_ready=true creates it prd-ready for auto-pickup by lisa:intake; default is draft for the Plan flow / human review. ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-research.md))
- **lisa-review-local**: Review local branch changes compared to main ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-review-local.md))
- **lisa-security-zap-scan**: Run an OWASP ZAP baseline security scan locally using Docker ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-security-zap-scan.md))
- **lisa-setup-atlassian**: Set up Atlassian (cloudId + acli profile) for this project. Writes the `atlassian` section of `.lisa.config.json` and enables the Atlassian MCP and/or installs acli as needed. Prerequisite for /lisa:setup:jira and /lisa:setup:confluence. ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-setup-atlassian.md))
- **lisa-setup-confluence**: Set up Confluence as the PRD source for this project. Writes the `confluence` section of `.lisa.config.json` (spaceKey and/or parentPageId) and offers to set top-level `source: "confluence"`. Depends on /lisa:setup:atlassian. ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-setup-confluence.md))
- **lisa-setup-github**: Set up GitHub Issues as the tracker and/or PRD source for this project. Verifies the gh CLI, resolves org/repo, scaffolds the `status:*` (build) and/or `prd-*` (PRD) label namespaces, writes the `github` section of `.lisa.config.json`, and can document optional GitHub ProjectV2 coordination before offering top-level `tracker: "github"` / `source: "github"`. No /lisa:setup:atlassian prerequisite. ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-setup-github.md))
- **lisa-setup-jira**: Set up JIRA as the tracker for this project. Writes `jira.project` and offers to set top-level `tracker: "jira"` in `.lisa.config.json`. Depends on /lisa:setup:atlassian (needs cloudId). ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-setup-jira.md))
- **lisa-setup-linear**: Set up Linear as the tracker and/or PRD source for this project. Verifies Linear access (MCP OAuth or a personal API key in keychain), resolves the workspace slug and team key, scaffolds the `status:*` issue-label (build) and/or `prd-*` project-label (PRD) namespaces, writes the `linear` section of `.lisa.config.json`, and offers to set top-level `tracker: "linear"` / `source: "linear"`. No /lisa:setup:atlassian prerequisite. ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-setup-linear.md))
- **lisa-setup-notion**: Set up Notion as the PRD source for this project. Walks the user through creating a workspace-scoped internal-integration token, sharing the PRD database with it, and stores the token in OS keychain. Writes `notion.workspaceId`, `notion.prdDatabaseId`, and `notion.values` into `.lisa.config.json`. Offers to set top-level `source: "notion"`. ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-setup-notion.md))
- **lisa-sync-down**: Back-sync an environment branch down the deploy chain (hotfix propagation) on demand ([CodySwannGT/grist](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/grist/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/lisa-sync-down.md))
